false, false, false];
    private static ulong _result;

    public static int Run(int input)
    {
        _result = 0;
        return Widths[input] == 32 ? SingleOperation(input) : DoubleOperation(input);
    }

    private static int SingleOperation(int input)
    {
        var left = BitConverter.UInt32BitsToSingle((uint)Left[input]);
        var right = BitConverter.UInt32BitsToSingle((uint)Right[input]);
        var operation = Operations[input];
        if (operation == 6)
        {
            _result = (ulong)(MathF.Sign(left) + 1);
            return _result == Expected[input] ? 42 : -1;
        }
        var result = operation switch
        {
            0 => MathF.Min(left, right),
            1 => MathF.Max(left, right),
            2 => MathF.CopySign(left, right),
            3 => MathF.BitIncrement(left),
            4 => MathF.BitDecrement(left),
            _ => MathF.Abs(left),
        };
        if (ExpectNaN[input])
        {
            _result = float.IsNaN(result) ? 1UL : 0;
            if (_result != 1) return -2;
            if (operation == 2 && float.IsNegative(result) != ExpectNegative[input]) return -3;
            return 42;
        }
        _result = BitConverter.SingleToUInt32Bits(result);
        return _result == Expected[input] ? 42 : -4;
    }

    private static int DoubleOperation(int input)
    {
        var left = BitConverter.UInt64BitsToDouble(Left[input]);
        var right = BitConverter.UInt64BitsToDouble(Right[input]);
        var operation = Operations[input];
        if (operation == 6)
        {
            _result = (ulong)(Math.Sign(left) + 1);
            return _result == Expected[input] ? 42 : -1;
        }
        var result = operation switch
        {
            0 => Math.Min(left, right),
            1 => Math.Max(left, right),
            2 => Math.CopySign(left, right),
            3 => Math.BitIncrement(left),
            4 => Math.BitDecrement(left),
            _ => Math.Abs(left),
        };
        if (ExpectNaN[input])
        {
            _result = double.IsNaN(result) ? 1UL : 0;
            if (_result != 1) return -2;
            if (operation == 2 && double.IsNegative(result) != ExpectNegative[input]) return -3;
            return 42;
        }
        _result = BitConverter.DoubleToUInt64Bits(result);
        return _result == Expected[input] ? 42 : -4;
    }

    public static int Trace() => 1;
    public static int TraceCount() => 1;
    public static int TraceKind(int index) => 3;
    public static int TraceEventId(int index) => index;
    public static int TracePayloadLow(int index) => unchecked((int)_result);
    public static int TracePayloadHigh(int index) => unchecked((int)(_result >> 32));
}
